      "content": "**Key instructions**:\n(1) Make sure to end code blocks with ``` followed by a newline(\n).\n(2) Remember you can use the variables in your code in subsequent code blocks.\n(3) Remember that the email addresses, access tokens and variables (e.g. spotify_password) in the example above are not valid anymore.\n(4) You can use the \"supervisor\" app to get information about my accounts and use the \"phone\" app to get information about friends and family.\n(5) Always look at API specifications (using apis.api_docs.show_api_doc) before calling an API.\n(6) Write small chunks of code and only one chunk of code in every step. Make sure everything is working correctly before making any irreversible change.\n(7) Many APIs return items in \"pages\". Make sure to run through all the pages by looping over `page_index`.\n(8) Once you have completed the task, make sure to call apis.supervisor.complete_task(). If the task asked for some information, return it as the answer argument, i.e. call apis.supervisor.complete_task(answer=<answer>).       "content": "I am your supervisor and you are a super intelligent AI Assistant whose job is to achieve my day-to-day tasks completely autonomously.\n\nTo do this, you will need to interact with app/s (e.g., spotify, venmo etc) using their associated APIs on my behalf.   "prompt_tokens": 9408,
  "raw_model_response": "I have successfully retrieved my friends' email addresses. Now I can use the `show_transactions` API to get all transactions of the ongoing month to and from my friends. Let me start by getting the first page of transactions.\n\nCode:\n```python\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\npage_limit = 20\nwhile True:\n    transaction_page = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=a890896e3998dc13c73c4aeadc2e2b99a7f6a413139d600940a56d599961b1df>,\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        user_email=friends_emails,\n        page_index=page_index,\n        page_limit=page_limit\n    )\n    if not transaction_page:\n        break\n    transactions.extend(transaction_page)\n    page_index += 1\n\nprint(f\"Total transactions: {len(transactions)}\")\n```",

  "raw_model_response": "I see the error - the `max_created_at` format is incorrect. Let me fix the date format and try again.\n\nCode:\n```python\n# Correct the max_created_at format to YYYY-MM-DD\nmax_created_at = max_created_at.strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n\ntransactions = []\npage_index = 0\nwhile True:\n    transaction_page = apis.venmo.show_transactions(\n        access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=a890896e3998dc13c73c4aeadc2e2b99a7f6a413139d600940a56d599961b1df>,\n        min_created_at=min_created_at,\n        max_created_at=max_created_at,\n        page_index=page_index\n    )\n    if not transaction_page:\n        break\n    transactions.extend(transaction_page)\n    page_index += 1\n\n# Filter transactions to and from friends\nfiltered_transactions = []\nfor transaction in transactions:\n    sender_email = transaction['sender']['email']\n    receiver_email = transaction['receiver']['email']\n    if sender_email in friend_emails or receiver_email in friend_emails:\n        filtered_transactions.append(transaction)\n\nprint(f\"Filtered transactions: {len(filtered_transactions)}\")\n```",

  "complete_environment_observation": "<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL_OBSERVATION:SHA256=893e75a4b2a311f8352fcdd7f2742d70db1e1b4e0fea4d0ba8bacd1e1a4eb152>",
  "exact_executed_code": "apis.supervisor.complete_task()",
  "exact_model_message_array": [
    {
      "content": "I am your supervisor and you are a super intelligent AI Assistant whose job is to achieve my day-to-day tasks completely autonomously.\n\nTo do this, you will need to interact with app/s (e.g., spotify, venmo etc) using their associated APIs on my behalf. For this you will undertake a *multi-step conversation* using a python REPL environment. That is, you will write the python code and the environment will execute it and show you the result, based on which, you will write python code for the next step and so on, until you've achieved the goal. This environment will let you interact with app/s using their associated APIs on my behalf.\n\nHere are three key APIs that you need to know to get more information\n\n# To get a list of apps that are available to you.\n\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_app_descriptions())\n```\n\n# To get the list of apis under any app listed above, e.g. spotify\n\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_descriptions(app_name='spotify'))\n```\n\n# To get the specification of a particular api, e.g. spotify app's login api\n\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_doc(app_name='spotify', api_name='login'))\n```\n\nEach code execution will produce an output that you can use in subsequent calls. Like each transaction using the `like_transaction` API.\n\nLet me start by getting the current date to set the date range for the ongoing month.\n\nCode:\n```python\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\n\n# Get current date\ncurrent_date = datetime.now()\n\n# Set min_created_at to the first day of the current month\nmin_created_at = current_date.replace(day=1).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')\n\n# Set max_created_at to the last day of the current month\nif current_date.month == 12:\n    max_created_at = current_date.replace(year=current_date.year + 1, month=1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)\nelse:\n    max_created_at = current_date.replace(month=current_date.month + 1, day=1) - timedelta(days=1)\n\nprint(f\"min_created_at: {min_created_at}\")\nprint(f\"max_created_at: {max_created_at}\")\n```",

## Interpretation

- **VERIFIED:** task outcomes, actions, observations, and first divergences are from immutable raw rows.
- **INFERENCE:** N1-N2 differences are reported directionally; they are not presumed to be beneficial or memory-specific.
- **UNVERIFIED:** mechanisms downstream of the first recorded behavioral divergence.
